302	
303	/// The producing endpoint: `reserve` a slot, write in place,
304	/// `commit`.
305	pub struct Producer<'a> {
306	    /// The ring's control block.
307	    header: &'a Header,
308	    /// Base of the slot array.
309	    slots: *mut u8,
310	    /// Geometry snapshot (see [`Ring`]).
311	    slot_size: u32,
312	    /// Geometry snapshot (see [`Ring`]).
313	    capacity: u32,
314	    /// Slot-position mask (`capacity - 1`).
315	    mask: u32,
316	    _region: PhantomData<&'a [u8]>,
317	}
318	
319	// SAFETY: the handle owns the producer role; the shared state it
320	// touches (indices) is atomic, and slot writes are handed off
321	// with Release/Acquire ordering.
322	unsafe impl Send for Producer<'_> {}
323	
324	impl<'a> Producer<'a> {
325	    /// Reserve the next free slot as a `&mut T`, or [`Full`].
326	    ///
327	    /// - Dropping the guard without [`Reserved::commit`]
328	    ///   abandons the reservation (nothing published).
329	    pub fn reserve<T>(&mut self) -> Result<Reserved<'_, T>, Full>
330	    where
331	        T: FromBytes + IntoBytes + KnownLayout,
332	    {
333	        check_type::<T>(self.slot_size);
334	        let p = self.header.producer_idx.load(Ordering::Relaxed);
335	        let c = self.header.consumer_idx.load(Ordering::Acquire);
336	        // `>=`, not `==`: a peer-corrupted consumer_idx makes
337	        // occupancy look huge — fail Full, never hand out a
338	        // slot the protocol doesn't own.
339	        if p.wrapping_sub(c) >= self.capacity {
340	            return Err(Full);
341	        }
342	        let bytes = slot_ptr(self.slots, p, self.mask, self.slot_size);
343	        // SAFETY: index protocol gives the producer exclusive
344	        // access to this slot until commit; range validated at
345	        // init/attach.
346	        let bytes = unsafe { core::slice::from_raw_parts_mut(bytes, self.slot_size as usize) };
347	        #[allow(clippy::unwrap_used)]
348	        // OK: check_type guarantees size, slot base guarantees
349	        // alignment, so the prefix cast cannot fail.
350	        let (msg, _) = T::mut_from_prefix(bytes).unwrap();
351	        Ok(Reserved {
352	            header: self.header,
353	            msg,
354	            next_idx: p.wrapping_add(1),
355	        })
356	    }
357	}
358	
359	/// A reserved slot: `DerefMut` to write the message, then
360	/// [`commit`](Reserved::commit).
361	pub struct Reserved<'p, T> {
362	    /// The ring's control block (for the commit store).
363	    header: &'p Header,
364	    /// The slot, viewed as the message type.
365	    msg: &'p mut T,
366	    /// Value `producer_idx` takes on commit.
367	    next_idx: u32,
368	}
369	
370	impl<T> Deref for Reserved<'_, T> {
371	    type Target = T;
372	    /// Read access to the in-slot message.
373	    fn deref(&self) -> &T {
374	        self.msg
375	    }
376	}
377	
378	impl<T> DerefMut for Reserved<'_, T> {
379	    /// Write access to the in-slot message.
380	    fn deref_mut(&mut self) -> &mut T {
381	        self.msg
382	    }
383	}
384	
385	impl<T> Reserved<'_, T> {
386	    /// Publish the slot to the consumer
387	    /// (`producer_idx + 1`, `Release`).
388	    pub fn commit(self) {
389	        self.header
390	            .producer_idx
391	            .store(self.next_idx, Ordering::Release);
392	    }
393	}
394	
395	/// The consuming endpoint: `peek` the oldest unread slot, read
396	/// in place, `release`.
397	pub struct Consumer<'a> {
398	    /// The ring's control block.
399	    header: &'a Header,
400	    /// Base of the slot array.
401	    slots: *mut u8,
402	    /// Geometry snapshot (see [`Ring`]).
403	    slot_size: u32,
404	    /// Slot-position mask (`capacity - 1`).
405	    mask: u32,
406	    _region: PhantomData<&'a [u8]>,
407	}
408	
409	// SAFETY: the handle owns the consumer role; see the Producer
410	// Send rationale.
411	unsafe impl Send for Consumer<'_> {}
412	
413	impl<'a> Consumer<'a> {
414	    /// View the oldest unread slot as a `&T`, or [`Empty`].
415	    ///
416	    /// - Dropping the guard without [`Peeked::release`] leaves
417	    ///   the slot unread — the next peek returns it again.
418	    pub fn peek<T>(&mut self) -> Result<Peeked<'_, T>, Empty>
419	    where
420	        T: FromBytes + KnownLayout + Immutable,
421	    {
422	        check_type::<T>(self.slot_size);
423	        let c = self.header.consumer_idx.load(Ordering::Relaxed);
424	        let p = self.header.producer_idx.load(Ordering::Acquire);
425	        if p == c {
426	            return Err(Empty);
427	        }
428	        let bytes = slot_ptr(self.slots, c, self.mask, self.slot_size);
429	        // SAFETY: index protocol gives the consumer exclusive
430	        // access to this slot until release; range validated at
431	        // init/attach.
432	        let bytes =
433	            unsafe { core::slice::from_raw_parts(bytes as *const u8, self.slot_size as usize) };
434	        #[allow(clippy::unwrap_used)]
435	        // OK: check_type guarantees size, slot base guarantees
436	        // alignment, so the prefix cast cannot fail.
437	        let (msg, _) = T::ref_from_prefix(bytes).unwrap();
438	        Ok(Peeked {
439	            header: self.header,
440	            msg,
441	            next_idx: c.wrapping_add(1),
442	        })
443	    }
444	}
445	
446	/// A peeked slot: `Deref` to read the message, then
447	/// [`release`](Peeked::release).
448	pub struct Peeked<'c, T> {
449	    /// The ring's control block (for the release store).
450	    header: &'c Header,
451	    /// The slot, viewed as the message type.
452	    msg: &'c T,
453	    /// Value `consumer_idx` takes on release.
454	    next_idx: u32,
455	}
456	
457	impl<T> Deref for Peeked<'_, T> {
458	    type Target = T;
459	    /// Read access to the in-slot message.
460	    fn deref(&self) -> &T {
461	        self.msg
462	    }
463	}
464	
465	impl<T> Peeked<'_, T> {
466	    /// Free the slot for reuse (`consumer_idx + 1`, `Release`).
467	    pub fn release(self) {
468	        self.header
469	            .consumer_idx
470	            .store(self.next_idx, Ordering::Release);
471	    }
472	}
473	
474	#[cfg(test)]
475	mod tests {
476	    use super::*;
477	
478	    /// Test message; two words so a torn write would be visible.
479	    #[derive(FromBytes, IntoBytes, KnownLayout, Immutable, Debug, PartialEq)]
```

> AGENT

Work for `0.3.0-6` is complete. Both validations green: cargo cycle 6/6, and the **full** Miri suite 6/6 — including `threaded_spsc`, which is the test that reproduced your failure. Please review. What changed:

**The fix (`src/lib.rs`):**
- `Reserved` and `Peeked` now hold **raw slot pointers** (`*mut T` / `*const T`) plus a `PhantomData` borrow marker; `Deref`/`DerefMut` mint short-lived references per access, with the SAFETY argument (check_type size/align, `FromBytes` validity, index-protocol exclusivity). By the time `commit(self)`/`release(self)` runs, no reference into the slot exists to be argument-protected — the race Miri flagged is structurally gone.
- `reserve`/`peek` derive the pointer raw-to-raw from the slot base; the intermediate slice, the zerocopy prefix-cast calls, and their two `unwrap`s are deleted (the zerocopy trait bounds remain as the message contract).
- `threaded_spsc` runs `COUNT = 200` under `cfg(miri)` (100k natively), so the full suite is Miri-runnable — about 1 second.

**Docs (riding this commit):** the README `## Overview`/`## Testing` you asked for (Testing now documents plain `cargo +nightly miri test`, the two bugs Miri caught, and the reduced-count note), the design doc's Validation section updated to "full suite, no skip" plus a new *Guard internals* paragraph explaining why the guards are raw-pointer-based, and the notes/README pointers.

**Choreography:** the close-out-only edits are reverted for now — In Progress is back with `-6` marked `(current)`, the chores section is bare again, Cargo.toml is `0.3.0-6`. Close-out gets redone cleanly after `-6` lands, and ends up purely bookkeeping.

One process note worth recording at close-out: `--skip threaded` was a coverage hole — the only concurrency-exercising test was excluded from the only tool that checks aliasing rules. The Outcome section will say so.

Want the commit description?

> AGENT

**Title:** `fix: ring buffer guard aliasing under Miri`

**Body:**
```
Fix UB found by the user running the full Miri suite
(0.3.0-6): Reserved/Peeked held &mut T / &T fields, which are
argument-protected (noalias) for the whole commit()/release()
call — but the store inside that call hands the slot to the
other side, which can access it while the protector is live.
Guards now hold raw slot pointers and mint references per
access, so no slot reference exists when the handoff store
runs. Full Miri suite (threaded included) now passes; the
prior --skip threaded run was a coverage hole — the only
concurrency test was excluded from the only aliasing checker.

- src/lib.rs
  - Reserved/Peeked: msg is *mut T / *const T + PhantomData
    borrow marker; Deref/DerefMut mint short-lived refs with
    per-site SAFETY comments
  - reserve/peek: derive the slot pointer raw-to-raw; drop
    the intermediate slice, zerocopy prefix casts, and their
    unwraps (trait bounds stay as the message contract)
